## 例 750：高级感建筑销售海报模型展示

**来源：** [@ou_zhen599](https://x.com/ou_zhen599/status/2090338251861266714) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/aerlith-habitat-premium-architectural-poster)

![case750.jpg](images/case750.jpg)

```text
Create an ultra-premium architectural sales poster for a fictional real-estate brand called AERLITH HABITAT, combining more refined Transit polish with stronger Orbit generation flow. Vertical format, soft taupe-beige studio background, elegant diagonal sunlight from the upper-right, and one central tabletop concept presentation where an architectural maquette and white technical overlays become one unified product story. The image must feel like a flagship launch visual for a boutique residential project: calm, luxurious, intelligent, and globally editorial.

Main composition:
Keep a strict vertical hierarchy. In the lower-middle, place one exquisitely crafted physical house maquette on a square site plinth, with warm timber construction, pitched roof, fine glazing, and clean miniature landscape integration. In the upper half, float a sparse architectural drawing system: one floor plan, one small elevation or axonometric study, and a few elegant annotation clusters. Strengthen the Orbit direction by making the relationship between upper diagrams and lower model feel more actively connected, as if the concept above is descending and materializing into the maquette below. Use delicate white strategy lines, arrows, and environmental curves to visually bridge upper and lower zones with a more continuous generative flow.

Architecture:
Design a compact high-end retreat house or boutique nature residence with a steep double-pitched roof, warm timber structure, floor-to-ceiling glazing, and one cleanly articulated entry volume. The model must feel fully buildable and coherent. Around the lower model, include a few translucent abstract trees, tiny white scale figures, airflow and sunlight path arrows, and subtle topographic or water-path lines, all highly restrained and elegant.

Transit direction:
Make everything cleaner, lighter, and more internationally premium. Reduce annotation density, increase breathing room, soften any unnecessary visual noise, and keep typography and graphics more editorial than explanatory. The image should feel closer to a luxury architecture monograph cover or global real-estate launch poster than a technical board. The technical overlays must remain precise but visually airy.

Lighting:
Use soft natural directional light from the upper-right, with visible but restrained sunlight beams crossing the upper and middle area. Let the light create long elegant shadows over the table, subtle glow on the roof edges, and a precious warmth across the wooden model. Keep contrast sculptural but clean. The light should also reinforce the Orbit feeling, guiding the eye diagonally from the upper diagrams down into the physical model.

Material realism:
Render the maquette with exceptionally refined tactile detail: warm basswood or pale oak surfaces, crisp cut-card edges, thin acrylic glazing, subtle paper textures, matte site base, miniature landscaping, and a neat stack of wood or material samples at the lower-right edge of the table. The white overlay drawings must feel like fine architectural drafting, crisp and precise against the warm background.

Typography and branding:
Add one small original English project title in the upper-left, such as “SOLEN HAVEN,” with a tiny supporting subtitle and a few lines of refined architectural copy, all fully original. Keep all typography white, elegant, sparse, and secondary. Small original diagram labels may appear near the plan and environmental arrows, but they must remain minimal.

Graphic system:
Only white technical overlays: floor plan, tiny elevation/axon sketch, solar path icons, airflow arrows, circles, contour lines, and micro-annotations. Strengthen the sense that these graphics are not just hovering, but actively shaping and explaining the lower model. Keep them beautifully spaced and never cluttered.

Color system:
Warm taupe-beige background, honey wood tones, white drafting lines, pale translucent trees, soft gray shadows, and a subtle golden sunlight wash. Keep everything restrained, premium, and coherent.

Negative prompt:
no copied project title, no existing architect names, no Chinese title, no cluttered diagrams, no fantasy architecture, no distorted roof geometry, no messy material samples, no muddy shadows, no black blotches, no watermark, no existing logos, no noisy background, no random furniture clutter.
```

## 例 751：极简奢华VELORE覆盆子慕斯海报

**来源：** [@ou_zhen599](https://x.com/ou_zhen599/status/2090375698263494860) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/velore-minimalist-raspberry-mousse-poster)

![case751.jpg](images/case751.jpg)

```text
Create a premium minimalist dessert campaign poster for a fictional haute patisserie brand called VELORÉ, combining Transit-style restraint with Orbit-style poetic motion in a further purified composition. The image must feel ultra-clean, gallery-like, elegant, and commercially luxurious, with the dessert as the absolute and solitary visual hero. No copied text, no watermark, no clutter, no unnecessary props.

Main composition:
A single round raspberry mousse cheesecake sits perfectly centered on a matte white ceramic plate in the lower-middle of the frame. The cake is the only substantial object in the composition. Preserve a strict three-layer structure: a dark cocoa crumb base, a thick ivory mousse body with fine natural aeration, and a flawless glossy raspberry-red mirror glaze on top. The garnish is reduced to the minimum iconic expression: two fresh raspberries, one blackberry, one small whipped cream accent, and one extremely delicate blossom sprig rising upward in a graceful curved arc. The blossom line should create a subtle orbital motion that guides the eye upward and back toward the cake, while remaining almost weightless.

Transit restraint:
Use a large field of warm off-white negative space. Typography must be reduced to a highly disciplined editorial minimum: one small brand line, one product name, one short descriptor. Remove all extra annotation blocks and nonessential copy. The image should read instantly as a flagship luxury dessert poster.

Orbit motion:
The only motion cue comes from the elegant upward arc of the blossom sprig and the gentle clustered rhythm of the berries on top, creating a refined sense of lift and circulation above the cake. The motion must feel quiet, premium, and intentional, never decorative or busy.

Typography and layout:
Place a very small refined brand line at the upper center: “VELORÉ PATISSERIE”. Beneath it, set the product name in elegant editorial type: “Raspberry Cloud”. Add one restrained subline: “Silken mousse with vivid fruit glaze.” All typography should be dark charcoal, airy, centered, and clearly secondary to the cake.

Lighting:
Soft diffused studio light from the upper left-front, with subtle fill from the right and a very gentle shadow beneath the plate. Keep the light bright, soft, and sculptural, with a controlled highlight across the red glaze and delicate volume on the mousse body. No hard shadows, no muddy contrast, no overexposed whites.

Material rendering:
The raspberry mirror glaze must look ultra-smooth, glassy, and slightly viscous. The mousse layer should feel chilled, creamy, and naturally aerated with microscopic pores. The cocoa base should be compact, crisp, and finely granular. The berries must show realistic drupelet texture, subtle bloom, natural moisture, and fresh color depth. The whipped cream accent should feel dense, soft, and freshly piped. The blossom sprig must be fine, botanical, and fragile with tiny pink florets.

Color system:
Warm off-white, ivory cream, deep raspberry red, dark cocoa brown, blackberry black-purple, and a tiny accent of soft botanical pink. Keep the palette highly restrained, with the red glaze and berries as the only strong chromatic focus.

Style:
Luxury editorial dessert photography, ultra-minimal patisserie branding, flagship commercial food poster, Cannes-level premium restraint, clean, tactile, refined, and memorable.

Image quality and physical constraints:
Ultra-detailed, high resolution, crisp edges, correct circular geometry, clean plate silhouette, realistic glaze reflections, believable garnish balance, immaculate background, no extra crumbs, no random props, no distortion, no cheap bakery styling.

Negative prompt:
cheap bakery flyer, copied original text, cluttered layout, too many berries, excessive garnish, random props, cartoon dessert, fake CGI food, plastic glaze, waxy cream, distorted cake shape, dirty plate, harsh shadows, flat lighting, muddy whites, oversaturated red, noisy background, unreadable typography, watermark
```

## 例 752：极简几何艺术海报分屏设计

**来源：** [@xiaoxiaodong01](https://x.com/xiaoxiaodong01/status/2090415726813393008) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/minimalist-geometric-split-poster-design)

![case752.jpg](images/case752.jpg)

```text
Please turn each photo I upload into an independent high-end design poster, no multi-image stitching, each photo output individually. The overall composition adopts a 3:4 vertical layout, with the upper and lower areas strictly 1:1 in height, each occupying 50% of the screen.

The upper part preserves the original photo, maintaining the subject structure, real texture, natural light and shadow, and original color atmosphere, with only slight high-end photographic color grading to give it the exquisite texture of high-end brand advertising, art magazines, and exhibition visuals. To fit the frame, the sky, ground, or environmental background can be naturally extended, while the subject itself remains unchanged.

The lower part extracts the most recognizable subject, outline, posture, and narrative relationship from the photo, reconstructing it into a minimalist visual poster with high-end commercial aesthetics. The core language is "real objects passing through geometric emotional windows": a narrow, restrained, low-saturation geometric color block is set in the image as a visual anchor and spatial container. The direction of the color block is not fixed and can be horizontal, vertical, or diagonal, naturally determined by the subject's center of gravity, movement direction, outline trend, and layout rhythm. The subject enters the color block in a realistic photographic sense or exquisite realistic manner, and partially crosses boundaries, breaks the frame, extends out, or passes through borders, forming a sense of natural growth, spatial penetration, and white space breathing.

The composition remains minimalist, clear, and balanced, establishing order through geometric color blocks, subject boundary-crossing relationships, and large areas of white space. The subject can be centered, eccentric, growing vertically, or stretching horizontally, but always maintains a single focus. The background is mainly white, light gray, or very light warm colors, and very slight cultural patterns, linear graphics, topographic lines, water wave lines, light and shadow outlines, or abstract symbols can be added as faint auxiliary layers.

The color scheme extracts the most recognizable and energetic color relationships from the photo above, transforming them into a high-end system of light-colored geometric windows + real subject colors + a very small amount of neutral color typography. The color of the blocks can be softened into fog blue, light cyan, off-white, light pink, warm gray, light gold, or other light tones consistent with the original image's temperament, making the whole light, clean, modern, and exquisite.

Text naturally intervenes as part of the layout structure. A short English title can be extracted from the theme, emotion, action, state, or symbolic meaning of the photo, accompanied by a small number of subtitles, locations, state words, or miniature notes. The title can be arranged horizontally, vertically, or along the relationship between the color block and the subject. Auxiliary information is arranged neatly with small font size, thin weight, and stretched letter spacing, giving it a high-end editorial feel like high-end real estate, luxury goods, aesthetic magazines, or solar term visuals.

The overall temperament emphasizes Oriental white space, modern order, natural vitality, and high-end commercial aesthetic. Whether the subject is people, animals, plants, architecture, objects, food, vehicles, or natural landscapes, it should maintain a clear, elegant, and transferable visual echo between real photography and geometric emotional windows. ## 例 753：高端极简城市旅游海报设计
```

**来源：** [@AiwithLariab](https://x.com/AiwithLariab/status/2092642111502045372) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/premium-minimalist-travel-poster-design)

![case753.jpg](images/case753.jpg)

```text
Create a refined 3:4 vertical premium travel poster for [CITY].
First identify the city, country, architecture, landscape, climate, cultural character, and visual identity. Select ONE iconic and highly recognizable real-world landmark or subject as the HERO. Establish one believable photographic viewpoint and ensure every environmental element logically belongs to that location. Do not create a collage of unrelated landmarks.

COMPOSITION: Divide the poster into two perfectly equal sections: 50% top and 50% bottom. Use a warm beige, ivory, or soft-white background with generous negative space and precise alignment.

TOP — HERO PHOTOGRAPH: Create an ultra-realistic, high-end editorial photograph of the chosen landmark from a believable real-world viewpoint. Preserve accurate architecture, proportions, materials, surrounding environment, natural lighting, shadows, atmosphere, and geographic character. The scene should feel like premium architectural photography or a museum-quality travel photograph, with realistic textures and sophisticated cinematic composition.

BOTTOM — GRAPHIC INTERPRETATION: Reinterpret the same hero landmark as a sophisticated flat graphic design. Extract its most recognizable silhouette, contours, structural details, proportions, and distinctive architectural elements. Simplify them into elegant geometric shapes, flat color blocks, thin lines, restrained divisions, and generous negative space. The landmark must remain instantly recognizable without becoming a realistic illustration, 3D render, cartoon, or game graphic.

COLOR & TYPOGRAPHY: Derive a restrained palette of approximately 4–6 colors from the city's architecture, environment, and hero photograph. Keep the palette sophisticated with slightly muted saturation. Add minimal geometric accents only when they improve the composition.

Use limited, elegant typography such as: [CITY] [COUNTRY] ARCHITECTURAL STUDY 2026

Use Swiss/international graphic design, architectural exhibition posters, luxury editorial design, and museum visual identity as the aesthetic reference. Keep typography subtle, with generous letter spacing and strong visual hierarchy.

FINAL AESTHETIC: Sophisticated, minimal, contemporary, culturally authentic, artistic, and premium. The photographic upper half and graphic lower half should feel like two expressions of the same visual identity. The final result should resemble a world-class gallery poster, collector's travel print, architectural exhibition artwork, or luxury brand campaign.

Avoid landmark collages, inaccurate architecture, invented structures, excessive text, tourist-brochure aesthetics, neon, flashy effects, heavy gradients, glossy 3D rendering, cartoon styling, game UI, clutter, excessive decoration, and generic AI-generated cityscapes.
```


---

## 例 754：午夜旋涡万圣节限定饮品商业海报

**来源：** [@ou_zhen599](https://x.com/ou_zhen599/status/2092919810648633519) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/premium-halloween-midnight-swirl-beverage-poster)

![case754.jpg](images/case754.jpg)

```text
Create a premium Halloween limited-edition beverage poster for an original seasonal drink brand named "MIDNIGHT SWIRL". Use a hybrid visual language that combines ultra-realistic drink photography with clean cute flat illustration. Preserve the exact structural logic: a tall vertical poster, a saturated pumpkin-orange background, one oversized central iced blended drink as the absolute hero, one large irregular black silhouette shape behind the cup for contrast, one glowing pale moon disk in the upper-right background, small bat graphics on both sides, subtle spider-web corner motifs, and two to three adorable Halloween mascots near the lower sides. The final image must feel like a Cannes-level seasonal beverage campaign: playful, iconic, irresistible, graphic, and product-led.

Style lock:
Premium seasonal drink advertising, ultra-real product photography, cute Halloween illustration, clean retail-campaign composition, bold color blocking, polished FMCG hierarchy, launch-ready social media poster, no clutter.

Core composition:
Place one giant transparent plastic cup exactly in the center, front-facing and slightly enlarged so it dominates the entire poster. The cup must contain a striking black-and-orange marbled frozen beverage with rich internal swirl patterns visible through the clear plastic. Top it with a towering spiral of whipped cream that feels sculptural and generous, then drench it with thick glossy pumpkin-caramel syrup flowing in controlled ribbons down the cream and cup. Add crunchy dark cookie crumbs over the whipped cream and one small pumpkin-themed topper near the upper-right of the cream peak. Behind the cup, use a bold organic black shadow-like shape to intensify silhouette readability. Position the moon behind the upper-right of the drink, and place charming Halloween mascots near the lower-left and lower-right so they frame but never compete with the cup.

Port super-symbol logic:
Turn the drink itself into the brand’s unmistakable super-symbol. The combination of one clear cup, one black-orange marbled frozen core, one exaggerated whipped-cream tower, and one glossy caramel cascade must feel ownable and instantly recognizable even at thumbnail scale. The black-orange swirl must become the identity mark of the campaign, and the cream peak must read like a seasonal crown. The drink should feel like the entire Halloween brand myth condensed into one collectible object.

Orbit motion logic:
Create a strong circular reading path around the cup. The eye should begin at the whipped-cream peak, move through the caramel drips, descend into the black-orange marbled drink body, travel across the lower mascots and their cup gestures, rise through the bat flight arcs and moon curve, and then return into the hero cup silhouette. Use cream spiral direction, syrup flow, marbling curves, bat placement, moon edge, and mascot sightlines to create one elegant loop that repeatedly pulls attention back to the central drink.

Product design:
Design one irresistible signature beverage called "MIDNIGHT SWIRL FROST". The drink should feel like a premium coffee-dessert frappé made of black cocoa slush, dark espresso ice texture, pumpkin-caramel ribbons, airy whipped cream, and crunchy cookie crumble. The clear cup may carry original readable brand text and logo only on the packaging itself. The product must feel craveable, limited-edition, and commercially launch-ready.

Lighting:
Use bright premium studio lighting with soft frontal illumination and gentle top-left shaping. The cup, whipped cream, caramel syrup, and lid must be sharply modeled with clean specular highlights. Add a soft grounded shadow beneath the cup and mascots. Keep the background flat and graphic while the beverage remains dimensional and hyper-real.

Materials and realism:
Render the plastic cup with physically accurate transparency, cold condensation haze, moisture beads, realistic lid reflections, and crisp printed branding. Render the whipped cream with soft airy peaks, realistic dairy texture, and subtle fold shadows. Render the caramel-orange syrup with glossy viscosity, realistic drip weight, and appetizing shine. Render the dark beverage with dense frozen texture, fine ice crystals, black cocoa depth, espresso richness, and suspended orange swirl marbling. Render the cookie crumbs as irregular crunchy dark clusters.

Illustration system:
Use cute Halloween mascot illustrations with simple premium silhouettes and minimal shading: one pumpkin-headed character in a knitted beanie holding a mini drink, one skeleton-like character in a matching beanie sipping the beverage, and one rounded ghost character peeking from the edge if needed. Keep the illustration style clean, playful, and secondary to the product.

Color strategy:
Dominant colors: vivid pumpkin orange, deep black, creamy white. Secondary tones: caramel amber, dark cocoa brown, soft moon-cream. Accent tones: small green straw notes and muted gray costume details. Keep the palette bold, festive, premium, and instantly seasonal.

Typography and layout:
Use original English text only. Place a short playful title near the top center such as "SIP THE SHADOW" with a concise seasonal subline beneath it. Add one compact bottom badge strip with brief original flavor notes such as "BLACK COCOA + PUMPKIN CARAMEL + WHIPPED CREAM". Do not copy any existing brand name, slogan, or promotional wording.

Quality target:
Hyper-detailed, high-resolution commercial finish, pristine product sharpness, appetizing fluid realism, clean mascot integration, strong symbolic readability, premium seasonal branding, no clutter, no muddy blacks, no cheap holiday flyer look.

Negative prompt:
copied brand name, copied slogan, weak product hierarchy, blurry cup logo, muddy black drink, fake whipped cream, thin syrup, messy mascot style, overcrowded layout, low-resolution packaging, dull orange background, cheap Halloween party flyer aesthetic, unreadable text, warped cup perspective, dirty shadows, black blotches
```

## 例 755：复古极简中世纪风格旅游海报生成提示词

**来源：** [@Naiknelofar788](https://x.com/Naiknelofar788/status/2092581373102186567) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/vintage-minimalist-mid-century-travel-poster-prompt)

![case755.jpg](images/case755.jpg)

```text
Create a beautiful vertical 4:5 vintage travel poster for [COUNTRY], inspired by classic mid-century European travel posters and aged lithographic printmaking.

Instead of showing multiple landmarks, build the entire poster around one poetic visual scene that captures the soul of the country.

Feature a striking landscape associated with [COUNTRY] — dramatic mountains, coastline, countryside, old streets, desert, forests, lakes, or rolling hills — with one iconic cultural element subtly integrated into the scene.

Use a refined vintage color palette, slightly faded pigments, warm paper texture, subtle grain, imperfect ink edges, gentle shadows, and authentic screen-printed/lithographic details.

The composition should be minimal, balanced, and elegant, with large areas of negative space and a strong central visual. Add tiny atmospheric details such as birds, distant travelers, a train, bicycle, boat, or small village lights to give the scene life without making it crowded.

Typography should feel like a genuine 1950s–1970s tourism poster:

[COUNTRY]
Discover the Soul of [COUNTRY]

Use elegant vintage typography, carefully integrated into the composition.

The final artwork should feel like a rare old travel poster rediscovered in a European railway station — nostalgic, sophisticated, dreamy, collectible, cinematic, and timeless.

No modern graphics, no photorealistic collage, no excessive landmarks, no neon colors, no 3D text, no clutter.
Premium vintage print aesthetic, authentic paper aging, beautiful composition, artistic restraint.
```

## 例 756：星际迷航原初系列风格角色宣传海报提示词

**来源：** [@livybabie](https://x.com/livybabie/status/2092935742813081901) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/star-trek-tos-style-character-promotional-poster)

![case756.jpg](images/case756.jpg)

```text
Create a modern cinematic 4K promotional poster of [CHARACTER  NAME] from [SERIES/GAME], clearly portrayed as a mature adult woman aged 25+.

Preserve her unmistakable canonical identity: exact face, hairstyle, hair color, eyes, signature accessories, species traits, recognizable proportions and personality. Do not redesign her or turn her into a generic woman.

Place her aboard the USS Enterprise NCC-1701 bridge from Star Trek: The Original Series, preserving the iconic 1960s retro-futuristic aesthetic: circular bridge, Captain’s Chair, red railing, colorful analog control panels, illuminated buttons and main viewscreen showing deep space, planets or the Enterprise.

She wears the classic vintage female Starfleet TOS uniform: fitted long-sleeved mini-dress, black collar, authentic delta insignia, subtle gold rank braid and black knee-high boots. Choose gold command, blue science/medical or red operations/security according to what best matches her personality and visual identity.

Pose her confidently beside or near the Captain’s Chair, full-body or 3/4 body, elegant cinematic stance, expression strongly reflecting her canonical personality.

Vertical premium poster composition, modern 4K live-action cosplay realism, highly detailed skin, realistic individual hair strands, authentic vintage fabric texture, cinematic studio lighting, shallow depth of field, polished sci-fi promotional photography.

Add tasteful poster typography:
STAR TREK
THE ORIGINAL SERIES
[CHARACTER  NAME]
[OPTIONAL ROLE / TITLE]
USS ENTERPRISE NCC-1701

No redesign, no generic hairstyle, no altered species traits, no extra limbs, no distorted hands, no text errors.
```

## 例 757：极简主义粗野派时尚杂志封面海报

**来源：** [@woleswoosh](https://x.com/woleswoosh/status/2092673431951302950) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/high-end-brutalist-fashion-magazine-cover)

![case757.jpg](images/case757.jpg)

```text
A high-end brutalist magazine cover / fashion poster layout, portrait orientation. Close-up of a young East Asian woman with short, tousled dark hair featuring subtle teal-blue ends, soft natural skin texture, neutral expression with one eye open (clear blue-gray iris looking directly at the viewer) and the other eye softly closed in a gentle wink. Both hands raised framing her face, fingers lightly touching her temples and hair. The overall image is desaturated and almost monochromatic (cool gray tones), but a sharp white rectangular frame cuts across the eye area, restoring full subtle color and higher contrast only inside that rectangle (eyes, brows, and upper cheekbones remain slightly warmer and more vivid). Soft diffused studio lighting from the front-left, smooth gradient gray background, shallow depth of field focused on the face.

Typography and graphic overlays in clean modernist / brutalist style:
- Large white outlined uppercase “FOCUS” stretched across the top of the head, partially overlapping the hair.
- Top-right corner: stacked white uppercase text “FREE AI DESIGN STUDIO” with a high-contrast black-and-white QR code directly beneath it.
- Vertical white text running down the left side of the face, rotated 90 degrees: “it’s in the eyes. always in the eyes”
- Bottom-left caption in thin white sans-serif: “Pretty eyes.Pretty heart.Pretty soul.”
- Bottom-right: a standard black barcode with the code “K4ZUH4” printed underneath in small white text.

Overall aesthetic: minimalist brutalist editorial design, high-contrast graphic overlays on a soft photographic portrait, fashion-magazine cover composition, precise geometric framing, clean negative space, professional studio photography look, slightly cinematic color grading with restrained teal accents only in the hair and the framed eye zone. No extra decorative elements. Sharp, high-resolution, print-ready quality.
```

## 例 758：VELCARA奢华珠宝石榴概念广告海报

**来源：** [@ou_zhen599](https://x.com/ou_zhen599/status/2092578985108762652) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/velcara-luxury-jewelry-pomegranate-poster)

![case758.jpg](images/case758.jpg)

```text
Create a luxury flagship advertising poster for an original fine-jewelry brand named "VELCARA". Preserve the same iconic still-life structure: one whole mature pomegranate centered in the lower-middle as the sculptural hero pedestal, one exquisite yellow-gold charm bracelet draped across the fruit in a graceful horizontal arc, one refined English headline block in the upper center, and one slim premium feature strip along the bottom edge. The background must remain a deep dark garnet-to-black gradient with rich cinematic depth. The final image must feel like a Cannes-level luxury jewelry campaign: minimal, sensual, commanding, symbolic, and instantly memorable, with the bracelet as the absolute commercial focal point.

Style lock:
Ultra-realistic luxury jewelry macro photography, dark editorial still life, premium fashion-house campaign design, refined negative space, high-end print sharpness, rich romantic restraint, iconic product-led hierarchy.

Core composition:
Place one whole pomegranate large and centered in the lower-middle, occupying the visual throne without overwhelming the jewelry. Drape one fine yellow-gold charm bracelet across the upper front curve of the fruit from left to right, following the natural roundness of the pomegranate like a ceremonial coronation line. Suspend six to seven distinct charms along the chain, with one elongated marquise-cut crystal drop hanging lowest at the exact center as the main visual anchor. Keep the upper headline centered with generous negative space above, and the bottom information strip thin, elegant, and fully subordinate to the hero object.

Orbit motion logic:
Build a clear semi-circular eye path. The eye should begin at the upper headline, descend into the fruit crown, travel along the bracelet arc from left charm to central marquise pendant to right charm, then fall softly to the bottom feature strip before returning upward through the round silhouette of the pomegranate. Use chain curvature, charm spacing, highlight rhythm, and the fruit’s circular volume to create one elegant orbit that repeatedly pulls attention back to the center pendant and the gold arc. The motion must feel luxurious, controlled, and inevitable.

Port super-symbol logic:
Turn the single pomegranate wearing a gold charm bracelet into the brand’s unmistakable super-symbol. The fruit must feel like a royal jewel altar or crowned relic rather than a simple prop. The bracelet should read as a coronation band, and the central pendant should feel like the seal of preciousness. Together, the dark crimson fruit and luminous gold arc must form an ownable luxury icon readable instantly even at small scale.

Lighting:
Use soft upper-front-left studio lighting with subtle right-side fill and delicate rim separation. Create smooth highlight rolloff on the pomegranate skin, rich micro-specular glints on the gold links, and crisp premium sparkle on the crystal charms. Shadows must stay feathered, elegant, and clean, with no dead black patches and no muddy transitions.

Materials and realism:
Render the pomegranate with hyper-real botanical texture: taut leathery skin, natural pores, slight dryness, crown detail, organic mottling, deep crimson-to-wine tonal variation, and believable irregular surface sheen. Render the bracelet in polished 18k yellow gold with realistic chain weight, precise link geometry, flawless charm attachment, and refined handcrafted finish. The charms must include varied silhouettes such as pear drops, medallions, celestial forms, and one central marquise-cut crystal, all with physically accurate reflections, gemstone transparency, and premium brilliance.

Color strategy:
Dominant colors: deep garnet, burgundy, blackened wine red, and dark cherry shadow. Secondary tones: rich yellow gold, antique amber, and muted bronze warmth. Accent tones: clear crystal white and soft champagne highlights. Keep the palette dark, regal, warm, and restrained, so the jewelry becomes the brightest visual punctuation.

Typography and layout:
Use an original all-English luxury headline in the upper center, such as "CROWN THE RARE", with a very short refined subline if needed. Place an original premium bottom strip with concise phrases such as "FINE CHARMS  •  18K GOLD  •  HAND-FINISHED". All text must be original, elegant, minimal, and globally marketable. Do not copy any existing brand name, slogan, or campaign wording.

Concept atmosphere:
No extra props, no sliced fruit, no juice, no clutter. The image should evoke abundance, coronation, rarity, and sacred adornment. The pomegranate must feel monumental and symbolic; the bracelet must feel precious, ceremonial, and irresistibly collectible.

Quality target:
Hyper-detailed, high-resolution luxury print finish, macro-grade jewelry clarity, flawless gold rendering, realistic crystal brilliance, unified lighting physics, elegant tonal depth, strong iconic readability, premium editorial polish.

Negative prompt:
copied brand name, copied slogan, copied layout text, weak bracelet visibility, oversized fruit dominance, dull gold, plastic-looking crystals, blurry chain, broken charm spacing, cluttered composition, sliced fruit, juice splash, extra props, muddy background, dead black patches, low-resolution detail, warped charms, inconsistent reflections, unreadable typography, cheap e-commerce styling
```

## 例 760：德州特色美食推广海报提示词

**来源：** [@Gdgtify](https://x.com/Gdgtify/status/2089729532354039889) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/texas-state-famous-dinner-promo-recipe-graphic)

![case760.jpg](images/case760.jpg)

```text
2x2 grid, 16:9, do this for 4 texas or other state specific famous dinners: INPUT = [DISH / SEASON_OR_OCCASION / FLAVOR_PROFILE / INGREDIENT_MODE / AUDIENCE] 

Run INDULGENT_RECIPE_PROMO_EXTRACTION:

DISH_DNA := infer category, texture, serving vessel, portion style, visual structure, flavor cues, and emotional appeal

OCCASION_DNA := infer holiday, party, weekday meal, family dinner, luxury dessert, picnic, brunch, sports night, date night, wellness routine, or cultural context

HERO_FOOD := infer best appetizing presentation:
scoop lift, slice pull, pour, steam, drizzle, fork bite, spoonful, cross-section, stacked layers, melting, crunch break, garnish fall, or glass pour

TITLE_SYSTEM := infer bold headline, descriptive modifier, flavor-forward name, and short craveable subtitle

INGREDIENT_SYSTEM := infer core ingredients, packaging vs fresh items, plus signs, labels, preparation notes, and substitutions if useful

CALLOUTS := infer badge claims:
easy, make-ahead, crowd-pleaser, no-bake, high-protein, cozy, spicy, crunchy, creamy, refreshing, budget-friendly, kid-friendly, etc.

MOOD_PROPS := infer contextual props from occasion:
seasonal produce, utensils, napkins, candles, table surface, cultural objects, party decor, kitchen tools, or none

ICON_STRIP := infer 3–5 benefits or use cases with simple icons

RENDER_STYLE := infer commercial food photography, editorial recipe card, social ad, cookbook spread, or packaging-inspired promo

Render:
A delicious promotional recipe graphic where the food, ingredients, copy, and props all come from the input dish.

Forbidden:
hard-coded pumpkin dessert, fixed whipped topping, fixed cake mix, fixed caramel drizzle, fixed fall props, copied brand packaging, fake ingredient claims.
```

## 例 761：创意突破边界高端产品广告大片

**来源：** [@aziz4ai](https://x.com/aziz4ai/status/2089985351930544254) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/premium-product-boundary-break-campaign)

![case761.jpg](images/case761.jpg)

```text
Use the uploaded product image as the single source of truth.

Create one premium 16:9 campaign image where the product appears inside a physical ad surface such as a poster, framed print, billboard, lightbox, screen, or magazine page, and one meaningful part of the same product breaks beyond that boundary into real space.

The boundary break must prove the product’s real benefit. The product stays fully accurate and unchanged. There is only one product, and the inside/outside portions must connect perfectly.

Choose the strongest benefit, one clear boundary, one escaping feature, and one real-world interaction. Make the viewer instantly understand where the flat ad ends and real space begins.

Preserve the product exactly: shape, proportions, logo, packaging, typography, colors, materials, textures, and all visible details. Do not redesign, recolor, distort, duplicate, or invent features.

Create a clean, high-end editorial composition with bold framing, refined negative space, cinematic lighting, accurate perspective, realistic contact shadows, tactile materials, and strong depth.

Add the authentic brand logo naturally and one original two-word headline matching the brand tone.

Output one visually striking standalone image only.

16:9. No grid, no collage, no split screen, no miniature world, no duplicated product, no fantasy portal, no random tearing, no unrelated props, and no random text.
```

## 例 762：现代扁平几何中式大字海报

**来源：** [@xiaoxiaodong01](https://x.com/xiaoxiaodong01/status/2087173607227367874) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/modern-flat-geometric-chinese-character-poster)

![case762.jpg](images/case762.jpg)

```text
Build a modern flat graphic around any chosen subject object, with a huge Chinese theme character, geometric color blocks, and a single-line organic contour interlacing through one another. Keep an overall bright, clean background field, letting approximately two-thirds or more of the space remain empty, and concentrate the main weight in an off-center cluster: a saturated rectangular block in the theme's primary color first establishes a stable plane; a deeper, low-lightness, cool-toned circle overlaps from beneath it and extends beyond the boundary; the core Chinese theme character advances vertically in oversized, heavy, geometric black strokes across the white background, rectangle, and circle, with partial occlusion and cropping creating a strong scale jump; on the left, the date and a bilingual subtitle form a compact numeric column, while on the right, short vertical sentences set in two stroke-weight tiers balance the weight; a few miniature captions, short dashes, and linear symbols scatter along the edges, creating a three-level reading rhythm from main character to side notes to details. Abstract the dynamic relationship derived from the theme into one continuous organic contour, drawn as a broad sweeping loop with a clear, near-black single line that enters from the large empty field, passes through the color blocks, and crosses the edge; the curve intersects itself several times while remaining legible as a single path; keep most of its interior as the underlying background color, adding only localized, transparent, fading fills in the theme's primary color at the tips and extended sections, so the light line creates tension with the heavy giant character. Adapt the color scheme to the theme: let a clean, high-lightness neutral background carry the largest area; keep the theme's primary color vivid and highly saturated, using it for the rectangle and the localized transparent gradient; use a deep cool support color for the circle, separated in hue from the primary color and blending softly into the primary color at its end; use near-black for text and the contour, with a very small amount of light text reversed onto the primary color block; fix unequal areas, lightness hierarchy, and saturation differences, but do not fix specific color names. Render all elements as flat surfaces like matte digital printing, with sharp contour edges and gradients only as two localized, slowly feathered transitions; do not add dimensional shadows, paper grain, or global lighting effects. Keep typography in a low-contrast, broad, thick, near-geometric black sans-serif skeleton; allow the main character's parts to be elongated, offset, cut out, and switched between color-block backgrounds; give numbers a rounded futuristic structure and compress small text with narrow letterforms and loose tracking. Replace brand names, advertising copy, and concrete dates with new theme information. Preserve in the final result the extreme whitespace, the geometric cluster positioned lower-center, the opposing pull between horizontal and vertical text, and the moment an organic line crosses rigid geometric shapes, while avoiding evenly filling every part or reducing the contour to an isolated decoration.
```

## 例 763：高端时尚编辑海报设计指南

**来源：** [@MrLarus](https://x.com/MrLarus/status/2086826696666132506) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/high-fashion-editorial-poster-design-prompt)

![case763.jpg](images/case763.jpg)

```text
Create a 9:16 high-fashion editorial poster.

Subject: [male / female / fashion model / artist]
Outfit: [fashion styling]
Palette: [red / black / white / custom palette]
Main title: [TITLE]
Design direction: [perspective × typography / blur × sharp detail / profile × oversized type / gesture × editorial layout]
Supporting text: [short phrase]

Build the poster around one strong photographic subject and one dominant graphic idea.

Use oversized typography as part of the composition rather than simple text placed around the image. Let letters interact with the subject through cropping, overlap, foreground/background layering, or scale contrast.

Depending on the selected direction:

• Perspective × typography — use a strong foreground gesture or body movement toward the camera, with dramatic near-far perspective and large type framing the action.

• Blur × sharp detail — keep parts of the portrait softly blurred while one narrow facial region remains extremely sharp, creating controlled focus contrast.

• Profile × oversized type — use a sculptural side-profile portrait against a bold flat-color background, with large serif typography partially hidden behind or crossing the silhouette.

• Gesture × editorial layout — combine an expressive fashion pose, subtle motion echo or secondary silhouette, and integrated magazine-style typography.

Keep the layout clean and visually bold. Use one dominant color, one neutral base, and limited accent colors. Typography should include one large headline, one short supporting line, and only a few small editorial labels.

Maintain realistic skin texture, natural facial anatomy, refined fashion styling, crisp fabric details, controlled studio lighting, strong negative space, and premium magazine-cover quality.
```

## 例 764：ZESTORA柑橘气泡饮高端商业海报

**来源：** [@AiwithBloodline](https://x.com/AiwithBloodline/status/2087105171205861535) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/zestora-citrus-sparkling-drink-commercial-poster)

![case764.jpg](images/case764.jpg)

```text
Create an ultra-premium commercial poster for an original beverage brand called ZESTORA, designed as a Cannes-level advertising key visual with one hyper-realistic citrus drink bottle as the absolute hero product and a bold upward explosion of layered fruit-splash shapes behind it. The composition must be a tall vertical poster with the bottle rising from the lower center, while a large radiant burst of sculpted yellow-orange splash forms expands upward and outward from the bottle mouth like a stylized flavor detonation. The overall image must feel bright, joyful, refreshing, graphic, highly controlled, and instantly memorable, combining hyper-real product realism with premium dimensional paper-sculpture aesthetics.

Core composition:
Use a strong centered composition. Place one realistic beverage bottle in the lower-center foreground, upright and slightly monumental, occupying the lower third of the poster. From the bottle opening, a compact creamy-citrus liquid burst rises directly upward and transitions into a large layered splash universe filling the upper two-thirds of the frame. This splash universe must feel like a series of overlapping abstract citrus-blast shapes, radiating outward in a clean symmetrical-organic arrangement. The bottle is the clear visual anchor; the splash world is the brand energy halo.

Product design:
Design the bottle as a globally market-ready premium citrus sparkling drink. Use the original brand name ZESTORA. The bottle must feel extremely real: transparent PET bottle, crisp highlights, subtle surface reflections, believable label wrap, condensation droplets, luminous orange-gold liquid inside, accurate cap threading, and tactile packaging detail. The label should use a refined bold logo system in deep cobalt, white, citrus orange, and leaf green accents, with a fresh premium international soda identity. The bottle should feel collectible, sharp, cold, and irresistibly drinkable.

Splash and shape system:
The explosion behind the bottle must be built from layered three-dimensional splash silhouettes in shades of lemon yellow, mango yellow, golden orange, and pale cream. These forms should look like sculpted paper-cut liquid clouds or premium molded graphic splash shapes, not random messy splatter. The splash cluster must begin tighter at the bottle mouth and grow broader and more dramatic as it rises upward. Keep edges rounded, clean, dimensional, and premium. The overall burst must feel playful and powerful, like citrus flavor physically blooming into space.

Fruit and garnish elements:
Scatter art-directed citrus wedges, small ring slices, and fresh green leaves around the splash composition. Use orange slices, lemon wedges, citrus rings, and a few small circular droplets. These elements must feel carefully placed, more designed than chaotic, orbiting the central splash with rhythm and balance. The fruit pieces should look premium, slightly stylized, and dimensional, matching the crafted poster world while still keeping believable citrus texture. Green leaves should add contrast and freshness, but remain clean and sparse enough to preserve visual sophistication.

Background:
Use a rich saturated orange background with subtle material texture, like luxury matte paper or premium embossed poster stock. The background must include softly layered tonal variation and a few recessed splash silhouettes so the entire frame feels immersive and designed, but not cluttered. Keep the atmosphere bright, warm, sunny, and refreshing.
```
